scalars t

vectors x(2), uq(12), v(3), w(15), uhat(4), uext(4), n(2), tau(1), mu(14), eta(4)

jacobian uq, w, uhat

hessian

batch x, uq, v, w, uhat, n, uext

outputs Flux, Source, Tdfunc, Ubou, Fbou, FbouHdg, Materialstate, Avfield, Initu, VisScalars, SurfaceQuantities

function Flux(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(f) = 8;
  f[0] = uq[1]+uq[4]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]);
  f[1] = (uq[1]*uq[1])/uq[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[5]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]);
  f[2] = (uq[1]*uq[2])/uq[0]+uq[6]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]);
  f[3] = (uq[1]*(uq[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uq[0]+uq[7]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[7]-(uq[3]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[1]*(uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[2]*(uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0]))-(uq[4]*w[8])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uq[2]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0])+(mu[5]*uq[1]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0]);
  f[4] = uq[2]+uq[8]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]);
  f[5] = (uq[1]*uq[2])/uq[0]+uq[9]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]);
  f[6] = (uq[2]*uq[2])/uq[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[10]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]);
  f[7] = (uq[2]*(uq[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uq[0]+uq[11]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[11]-(uq[3]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[1]*(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[2]*(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0]))-(uq[8]*w[8])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uq[1]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0])-(mu[5]*uq[2]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0]);
end

function Source(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(s) = 4;
  s[0] = 0.0;
  s[1] = 0.0;
  s[2] = 0.0;
  s[3] = 0.0;
end

function Tdfunc(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(m) = 4;
  m[0] = 1.0;
  m[1] = 1.0;
  m[2] = 1.0;
  m[3] = 1.0;
end

function Ubou(x, uq, v, w, uhat, n, tau, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(ub) = 32;
  ub[0] = eta[0];
  ub[1] = eta[1];
  ub[2] = eta[2];
  ub[3] = eta[3];
  ub[4] = uq[0]+((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[5] = uq[1]-((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(n[0]*(w[5]/mu[1]-(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+(n[1]*(n[0]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-n[1]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[6] = uq[2]-((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(n[1]*(w[5]/mu[1]-(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-(n[0]*(n[0]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-n[1]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[7] = uq[3]+((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(uq[3]/uq[0]-(w[5]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(mu[1]*uq[0])+(mu[2]*uq[0]*(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1])*(w[5]*w[5])-w[6]/(mu[2]*uq[0])))/(mu[3]*w[7])))/mu[2];
  ub[8] = uq[0];
  ub[9] = 0.0;
  ub[10] = 0.0;
  ub[11] = -uq[0]*((1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[1]*uq[1]))/2.0+(1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[2]*uq[2]))/2.0-uq[3]/uq[0]-(mu[7]-w[1])/(mu[3]*w[9]));
  ub[12] = uq[0];
  ub[13] = 0.0;
  ub[14] = 0.0;
  ub[15] = uq[3];
  ub[16] = uq[0];
  ub[17] = uq[1]-n[0]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  ub[18] = uq[2]-n[1]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  ub[19] = uq[3];
  ub[20] = uq[0];
  ub[21] = uq[1];
  ub[22] = uq[2];
  ub[23] = uq[3];
  ub[24] = eta[0];
  ub[25] = eta[1];
  ub[26] = eta[2];
  ub[27] = eta[3];
  ub[28] = uq[0]+((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[29] = uq[1]-((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(n[0]*(w[5]/mu[1]-(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+(n[1]*(n[0]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-n[1]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[30] = uq[2]-((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(n[1]*(w[5]/mu[1]-(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-(n[0]*(n[0]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-n[1]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0]))/mu[2];
  ub[31] = uq[3]+((mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[9]*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*(mu[8]-w[0])*(uq[3]/uq[0]-(w[5]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(mu[1]*uq[0])+(mu[2]*uq[0]*(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1])*(w[5]*w[5])-w[6]/(mu[2]*uq[0])))/(mu[3]*w[7])))/mu[2];
end

function Fbou(x, uq, v, w, uhat, n, tau, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(fb) = 32;
  fb[0] = n[0]*(eta[1]+uq[4]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]))+n[1]*(eta[2]+uq[8]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]))-tau[0]*(uhat[0]-eta[0]);
  fb[1] = n[1]*((eta[1]*eta[2])/eta[0]+uq[9]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*eta[2])/eta[0])/eta[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*eta[1])/eta[0])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))-tau[0]*(uhat[1]-eta[1])+n[0]*((eta[1]*eta[1])/eta[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[5]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*eta[1])/eta[0])*(4.0/3.0))/eta[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*eta[2])/eta[0])*(2.0/3.0))/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]));
  fb[2] = n[0]*((eta[1]*eta[2])/eta[0]+uq[6]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*eta[2])/eta[0])/eta[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*eta[1])/eta[0])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))-tau[0]*(uhat[2]-eta[2])+n[1]*((eta[2]*eta[2])/eta[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[10]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*eta[1])/eta[0])*(2.0/3.0))/eta[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*eta[2])/eta[0])*(4.0/3.0))/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]));
  fb[3] = n[0]*((eta[1]*(eta[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/eta[0]+uq[7]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[7]-(uq[4]*eta[3])/eta[0])/eta[0]+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*eta[1]*(uq[5]-(uq[4]*eta[1])/eta[0])+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*eta[2]*(uq[6]-(uq[4]*eta[2])/eta[0]))-(uq[4]*w[8])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*eta[2]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*eta[2])/eta[0])/eta[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*eta[1])/eta[0])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*eta[0])+(mu[5]*eta[1]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*eta[1])/eta[0])*(4.0/3.0))/eta[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*eta[2])/eta[0])*(2.0/3.0))/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*eta[0]))+n[1]*((eta[2]*(eta[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/eta[0]+uq[11]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[11]-(uq[8]*eta[3])/eta[0])/eta[0]+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*eta[1]*(uq[9]-(uq[8]*eta[1])/eta[0])+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*eta[2]*(uq[10]-(uq[8]*eta[2])/eta[0]))-(uq[8]*w[8])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*eta[1]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*eta[2])/eta[0])/eta[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*eta[1])/eta[0])/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*eta[0])-(mu[5]*eta[2]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*eta[1])/eta[0])*(2.0/3.0))/eta[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*eta[2])/eta[0])*(4.0/3.0))/eta[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*eta[0]))-tau[0]*(uhat[3]-eta[3]);
  fb[4] = n[0]*(uq[1]+uq[4]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]))+n[1]*(uq[2]+uq[8]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]))+tau[0]*(uq[0]-uhat[0]);
  fb[5] = n[1]*((uq[1]*uq[2])/uq[0]+uq[9]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))+tau[0]*(uq[1]-uhat[1])+n[0]*((uq[1]*uq[1])/uq[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[5]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]));
  fb[6] = n[0]*((uq[1]*uq[2])/uq[0]+uq[6]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))+tau[0]*(uq[2]-uhat[2])+n[1]*((uq[2]*uq[2])/uq[0]+w[0]/mu[2]+uq[10]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]));
  fb[7] = n[0]*((uq[1]*(uq[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uq[0]+uq[7]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[7]-(uq[3]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[1]*(uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[2]*(uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0]))-(uq[4]*w[8])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uq[2]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0])+(mu[5]*uq[1]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0]))+n[1]*((uq[2]*(uq[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uq[0]+uq[11]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[11]-(uq[3]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[1]*(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])+1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*uq[2]*(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0]))-(uq[8]*w[8])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uq[1]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[2]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[1]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0])-(mu[5]*uq[2]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uq[0]))+tau[0]*(uq[3]-uhat[3]);
  fb[8] = tau[0]*(uq[0]-uhat[0])+n[0]*uq[4]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+n[1]*uq[8]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13]);
  fb[9] = n[1]*(uq[9]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*(uq[6]/uq[0]+uq[9]/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))-tau[0]*uhat[1]+n[0]*(w[0]/mu[2]+uq[5]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])+(mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[5]*(4.0/3.0))/uq[0]-(uq[10]*(2.0/3.0))/uq[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]));

1]))*1.0E+2)/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])*(mu[2]*(uq[0]*uq[0])*w[5]*sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[0]*mu[1]*mu[3]*uq[1]*w[7]+n[1]*mu[1]*mu[3]*uq[2]*w[7])*(uq[3]/uq[0]-(w[5]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(mu[1]*uq[0])+(mu[2]*uq[0]*(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1])*(w[5]*w[5])-w[6]/(mu[2]*uq[0])))/(mu[3]*w[7])))/(mu[2]*2.0)-(mu[1]*1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*1.0/(w[5]*w[5])*tanh((w[5]*1.0E+2)/mu[1]+((n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0E+2)/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])*(-mu[2]*(uq[0]*uq[0])*w[5]*sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[0]*mu[1]*mu[3]*uq[1]*w[7]+n[1]*mu[1]*mu[3]*uq[2]*w[7])*(uq[3]/uq[0]+(w[5]*(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(mu[1]*uq[0])+(mu[2]*uq[0]*(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1])*(w[5]*w[5])-w[6]/(mu[2]*uq[0])))/(mu[3]*w[7])))/(mu[2]*2.0)))*(mu[8]-w[0])*(n[0]*(w[5]/mu[1]-(n[0]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0])*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+(n[1]*(n[0]*uq[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-n[1]*uq[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))/uq[0]))/(mu[2]*2.0));
end

function FbouHdg(x, uq, v, w, uhat, n, tau, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(fb) = 32;
  fb[0] = -uhat[0]+eta[0];
  fb[1] = -uhat[1]+eta[1];
  fb[2] = -uhat[2]+eta[2];
  fb[3] = -uhat[3]+eta[3];
  fb[4] = uq[0]-uhat[0];
  fb[5] = uq[1]-uhat[1];
  fb[6] = uq[2]-uhat[2];
  fb[7] = uq[3]-uhat[3];
  fb[8] = tau[0]*(uq[0]-uhat[0]);
  fb[9] = -tau[0]*uhat[1];
  fb[10] = -tau[0]*uhat[2];
  fb[11] = -tau[0]*(uhat[3]+uq[0]*((1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[1]*uq[1]))/2.0+(1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[2]*uq[2]))/2.0-uq[3]/uq[0]-(mu[7]-w[1])/(mu[3]*w[9])));
  fb[12] = uq[0]-uhat[0];
  fb[13] = -uhat[1];
  fb[14] = -uhat[2];
  fb[15] = n[0]*((uhat[1]*(uhat[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uhat[0]+uq[7]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[7]-(uq[4]*uhat[3])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[1]*(uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[2]*(uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0]))-(uq[4]*w[8])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uhat[2]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uhat[0])+(mu[5]*uhat[1]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uhat[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uhat[0]))+n[1]*((uhat[2]*(uhat[3]+w[0]/mu[2]))/uhat[0]+uq[11]*tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(mu[12]+v[1]*mu[13])-(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4])*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[11]-(uq[8]*uhat[3])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[1]*(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[2]*(uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0]))-(uq[8]*w[8])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[4])+(mu[5]*uhat[1]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uhat[0])-(mu[5]*uhat[2]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uhat[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]*uhat[0]))+tau[0]*(uq[3]-uhat[3]);
  fb[16] = tau[0]*(uq[0]-uhat[0])+n[0]*uq[4]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[8]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  fb[17] = -(n[0]*uq[5]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[9]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*((n[0]*n[0])/(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-1.0)+n[0]*(n[0]*uhat[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uhat[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-(n[0]*n[1]*(n[0]*uq[6]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[10]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  fb[18] = -(n[0]*uq[6]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[10]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*((n[1]*n[1])/(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-1.0)+n[1]*(n[0]*uhat[1]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uhat[2]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]))*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])-(n[0]*n[1]*(n[0]*uq[5]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[9]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])))/(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  fb[19] = tau[0]*(uq[3]-uhat[3])+n[0]*uq[7]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1])+n[1]*uq[11]*1.0/sqrt(n[0]*n[0]+n[1]*n[1]);
  fb[20] = n[0]*uq[4]+n[1]*uq[8]+tau[0]*(uq[0]-uhat[0]);
  fb[21] = n[0]*uq[5]+n[1]*uq[9]+tau[0]*(uq[1]-uhat[1]);
  fb[22] = n[0]*uq[6]+n[1]*uq[10]+tau[0]*(uq[2]-uhat[2]);
  fb[23] = n[0]*uq[7]+n[1]*uq[11]+tau[0]*(uq[3]-uhat[3]);

end

function Materialstate(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(state) = 2;
  state[0] = log(mu[0]*uq[0]);
  state[1] = -mu[3]*((1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[1]*uq[1]))/2.0+(1.0/(uq[0]*uq[0])*(uq[2]*uq[2]))/2.0-uq[3]/uq[0]);
end

function Avfield(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(f) = 2;
  f[0] = -(atan(mu[10]*-1.0E+3+tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(atan(tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0])*1.0E+3)/3.141592653589793+1.0/2.0)*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0])*1.0E+3+3.183097800805168E-1)/3.141592653589793+1.0/2.0)*(-mu[10]+tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(atan(tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0])*1.0E+3)/3.141592653589793+1.0/2.0)*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0])+3.183097800805168E-4)+tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*(atan(tanh(v[0]*mu[11])*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0])*1.0E+3)/3.141592653589793+1.0/2.0)*((uq[5]-(uq[1]*uq[4])/uq[0])/uq[0]+(uq[10]-(uq[2]*uq[8])/uq[0])/uq[0]);
  f[1] = w[0]/mu[2];
end

function Initu(x, eta, mu)
  output_size(ui) = 4;
  ui[0] = eta[0];
  ui[1] = eta[1];
  ui[2] = eta[2];
  ui[3] = eta[3];
end

function VisScalars(x, uq, v, w, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(s) = 1;
  s[0] = w[0]/mu[2];
end

function SurfaceQuantities(x, uq, v, w, uhat, n, tau, eta, mu, t)
  output_size(fb) = 3;
  fb[0] = ((mu[8]/mu[2]-w[0]/mu[2])*-2.0)/(eta[0]*(1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[1]*eta[1])+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[2]*eta[2])));
  fb[1] = ((n[1]*(-tau[0]*(uq[1]-uhat[1])+(n[1]*mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4])+(n[0]*mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uhat[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4]))+n[0]*(tau[0]*(uq[2]-uhat[2])-(n[0]*mu[5]*w[2]*((uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4])+(n[1]*mu[5]*w[2]*(((uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])*(2.0/3.0))/uhat[0]-((uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0])*(4.0/3.0))/uhat[0]))/(mu[0]*mu[1]*mu[4])))*2.0)/(eta[0]*(1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[1]*eta[1])+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[2]*eta[2])));
  fb[2] = ((tau[0]*(uq[3]-uhat[3])+(1.0/(mu[1]*mu[1]*mu[1])*mu[5]*(n[0]*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[7]-(uq[4]*uhat[3])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[1]*(uq[5]-(uq[4]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[2]*(uq[6]-(uq[4]*uhat[2])/uhat[0]))-(uq[4]*w[8])/uhat[0])+n[1]*(mu[3]*w[9]*(-(uq[11]-(uq[8]*uhat[3])/uhat[0])/uhat[0]+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[1]*(uq[9]-(uq[8]*uhat[1])/uhat[0])+1.0/(uhat[0]*uhat[0])*uhat[2]*(uq[10]-(uq[8]*uhat[2])/uhat[0]))-(uq[8]*w[8])/uhat[0]))*(w[3]+mu[6]*w[4]))/(mu[0]*mu[4]))*1.0/pow(1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[1]*eta[1])+1.0/(eta[0]*eta[0])*(eta[2]*eta[2]),3.0/2.0))/eta[0];
end
